Our key insight is that XTTSv2's voice cloning capabilities preserve prosodic structure independently of speaker identity, enabling voice conversion by conditioning on a pseudo-speaker. We introduce an iterative refinement strategy that balances privacy and utility by maximizing a harmonic mean of speaker dissimilarity and intelligibility. Evaluated on seven European languages across CommonVoice and Multilingual LibriSpeech, our system achieves near-optimal privacy (EER $\approx$ 0.49), competitive intelligibility, and substantially better speech quality than dedicated anonymization baselines, while requiring no language-specific training. We release the code here: https://github.com/rm00cr/coqui-tts.

</details>


### [202] [Puro-2B: Poor Lab's Qwen2-1.5B Trained on RTX 5090 within $5090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27370)
*Kairong Luo,Jiarui Cui,Yaorui Yin,Shengqi Chen,Yiming Yang,Linxiang Gao,Yanmohan Wang,Mingzhe Zhang,Kaiyue Wen,Kaifeng Lyu,Wenguang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套开放、低成本的大语言模型预训练配方（Puro-2B），使用消费级RTX 5090 GPU和FP8精度从头训练2B规模的模型，在1.4万亿tokens上实现不到$6.9K的训练成本，性能接近Qwen2.5-1.5B。论文还推导了Puro成本缩放定律，并研究了预训练数据课程对下游性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练成本极其高昂（如Llama-3.2-3B需$1.5M+，SmolLM3-3B需$700K+），远超学术和开源社区的承受范围。虽然已有开放权重模型和开源训练配方，但缺乏成本高效、硬件可及、真正开源可复现的预训练方案。

Method: 通过多种技术的组合实现成本降低：（1）选择消费级RTX 5090 GPU作为硬件平台；（2）采用FP8低精度训练；（3）hyperball优化；（4）课程模型平均（curriculum model averaging）；（5）精心设计的数据配方。在此基础上训练了Puro-2B模型系列，涵盖不同token预算和配方变体。

Result: 最佳模型在不到$6.9K的计算成本下完成训练，性能在评估协议下接近Qwen2.5-1.5B。推导出的Puro成本缩放定律表明约$4.4K（低于$5,090）即可达到Qwen2-1.5B的性能水平。此外，作为端到端案例研究，展示了预训练数据课程如何影响后训练后的下游性能。

Conclusion: 该工作显著降低了开源大模型预训练的经济门槛，提供了从数据、代码到模型权重的完整可复现配方（Apache 2.0许可），使更多研究者和社区成员能够参与大模型预训练研究。同时，完整的预训练管道访问权也使得以往难以进行的受控研究（如数据课程影响分析）成为可能。

Abstract: Language model pretraining has become almost synonymous with prohibitive cost, placing it out of reach for much of the academic and open-source communities. Although strong open-source efforts already exist, including open-weight models and open-source training recipes, a cost-efficient, hardware-accessible, and open-source pretraining recipe has long been missing. Even at a small scale, training Llama-3.2-3B costs over \$1.5M, and reproducing SmolLM3-3B needs over \$700K. In this report, we present an open pretraining recipe designed to lower this barrier. Using this recipe, we train a collection of Puro-2B models from scratch on up to 1.4 trillion tokens with FP8 precision on consumer-grade RTX 5090 GPUs. The models in the collection differ in token budgets and selected recipe variants. Our best model is trained at a compute cost of less than \$6.9K and approaches Qwen2.5-1.5B performance under our evaluation protocol. This cost efficiency is enabled by a combination of approaches, including hardware selection, low-precision training, hyperball optimization, curriculum model averaging, and the data recipe. Beyond the recipe itself, we provide two additional results. First, across the Puro-2B collection, we derive a Puro Cost Scaling Law that relates training cost to average model performance; the fitted law suggests that about \$4.4K, less than \$5,090, is sufficient to reach the performance of Qwen2-1.5B. Second, as an end-to-end case study, we examine how pretraining data curricula shape downstream performance after post-training. Such controlled studies are enabled by having access to the full pretraining pipeline rather than model weights alone. We release the full training recipe for Puro-2B, including data, code, and model weights under Apache 2.0 at https://huggingface.co/collections/thu-pacman/puro-2b.

</details>


### [203] [D2C-Routing: Dimension-to-Composition Evidence Routing for Mixed-Origin AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27380)
*Xin Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Yiqi Tong,Wei Guo,Yutian Xiao,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了维度到组合来源归因（Dimension-to-Composition Source Attribution）框架，将混合来源文本检测问题重新定义，并设计了D2C-Routing检测系统，在MixD2C基准上达到0.8603的四路Avg TPR@1%FPR，比同分片RACE-local重跑高出6.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI生成文本检测通常被建模为二元的文档级判断，但这种框架在混合来源写作场景下失效——文本的内容来源与表达来源可能不同（如AI生成内容但人类润色表达，或人类构思但AI表达）。因此需要更细粒度的来源归因方法来处理这种维度分离的情形。

Method: 提出Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing)方法：首先将内容侧和表达侧的证据分别路由到有监督的维度头（dimension heads），然后通过一个可学习的门控组合层（gated composition layer）将四类协作类型（content human/expression human, content human/expression AI, content AI/expression human, content AI/expression AI）组合，最终预测四路分类标签。

Result: 在MixD2C（从HART混合来源基准重建的划分）上，D2C-Routing检测系统达到0.8603的四路Avg TPR@1%FPR，比同分片RACE-local重跑高出6.5个百分点。核心消融实验支持路由设计；错误分析显示区分"AI内容/人类表达"与"完全AI生成"仍是最难判断的边界。

Conclusion: 维度到组合的来源归因框架和D2C-Routing方法有效解决了混合来源文本检测中二元分类失效的问题，通过内容与表达双维度证据分离及门控组合，显著提升了检测性能。未来仍需要加强对AI内容/人类表达与完全AI生成之间边界区分的建模能力。

Abstract: AI-generated text detection is commonly framed as a binary document-level judgment about whether a text is human-written or machine-generated. This framing breaks down for mixed-origin writing, where content origin and expression origin may differ. We cast mixed-origin detection as dimension-to-composition source attribution, inferring content origin and expression origin before composing them into four collaboration types. We propose Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing), which routes content-side and expression-side evidence to supervised dimension heads before a learned gated composition layer predicts the final label. On MixD2C, a reconstructed split derived from the HART mixed-origin benchmark, our disclosed D2C-Routing-based detector system reaches 0.8603 four-way Avg TPR@1%FPR, 6.5 points above the same-split RACE-local rerun. Core ablations support the routing design, while error analysis shows that distinguishing AI-content/human-expression from fully AI-generated text remains the hardest boundary. Code is available at https://github.com/bystander563/d2c-routing-artifact.

</details>


### [204] [RATIO: A Benchmark for Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations in Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27394)
*Maayan Sharon,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RATIO是一个大规模基准测试，定义了三种"创意动作"（Address、Broaden、Specify）来评估科学文献检索对灵感生成的帮助，基于数百万篇CS论文构建，实验表明特定操作的微调能显著提升检索器性能但仍存在改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献检索可以为人类和AI科学家提供灵感，但现有的检索基准只关注"直接解决问题"这一种相关形式，忽略了灵感可以来自不同抽象层次（宏观概括或具体实现）。缺乏一个能够评估不同层次灵感检索的系统性基准。

Method: 通过将轨迹标记远程监督（此前仅用于分类）扩展到语料规模检索，结合大规模LLM和人工审查，从数百万CS领域全文学术论文构建RATIO基准。定义三种创意动作：Address（检索问题解决方案）、Broaden（检索更泛化的表述）、Specify（检索具体实例），并采用操作特定微调训练检索器。

Result: 实验表明操作特定微调显著提升了检索器的性能，但整体仍存在较大改进空间，说明RATIO作为训练和评估框架能够支撑文献启发的创意生成，同时揭示了该任务的挑战性。

Conclusion: RATIO提供了一个可扩展的、支撑文献驱动创意的检索组件训练与评估框架，开辟了科学灵感检索这一新兴研究方向，为后续研究预留了进一步提升的空间。

Abstract: Retrieved scientific literature can serve as inspiration for both human and AI scientists. Inspiration can take different forms: prior work may directly suggest how to address a problem, or surface directions at different levels of abstraction - zooming out to a more general view or zooming in to a concrete realization. We introduce RATIO (Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations), a large-scale benchmark in which relevance is defined by three operations which we name ideation moves: Address retrieves potential approaches for stated problems, Broaden retrieves more general formulations, and Specify retrieves concrete instantiations. RATIO is constructed from millions of full-text scientific papers across CS literature via a general recipe that extends discourse-marker distant supervision - previously used only for classification - to corpus-scale retrieval, combined with extensive LLM and human vetting. Experiments show that operation-specific fine-tuning substantially boosts retrievers but leaves much room for further improvements. RATIO provides a scalable training and evaluation framework for retrieval components that support literature-grounded ideation, opening up new research avenues on scientific inspiration retrieval.

</details>


### [205] [Making Clinical Language Models Auditable: Concept-Guided Fine-Tuning for Robust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27397)
*Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAST框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）揭示Transformer中间激活中的稀疏可审计特征，结合LLM辅助解释管道和ICD-10检索约束标记SAE潜在因子，在微调期间通过残差减法抑制已验证的伪影潜在因子，并提供事后感知归因以审计模型决策，在MIMIC-IV死亡预测任务上优于对应的微调编码器基线并与强LLM基线保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在院内数据上表现优异，但在部署时因分布偏移而失效。核心原因是模型利用了笔记特定的伪影（如模板、分隔符、套话），这些伪影并不反映真实患者状态，导致模型在外部部署时泛化能力不足，亟需一种可审计的方法来识别并抑制这类伪影。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从Transformer中间激活中提取稀疏、人类可审计的特征；2) 通过LLM辅助解释管道结合ICD-10检索约束对SAE潜在因子进行语义标注；3) 在微调过程中通过残差减法抑制已验证的伪影潜在因子；4) 提供事后感知归因用于审计模型决策。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV出院记录死亡预测任务上，CAST相比对应的微调编码器基线取得改进，并与强LLM基线保持竞争力，同时能够生成特征级审计轨迹，展示支持每个预测的临床概念以及训练中抑制的伪影概念。

Conclusion: CAST框架通过SAE驱动的特征层面对齐与伪影抑制，同时兼顾模型性能提升与决策可审计性，为临床文本分类提供了一种可解释且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical language models can achieve strong in-hospital accuracy yet fail under deployment shifts because they exploit note-specific artifacts (e.g., templates, separators, boilerplate) that do not reflect patient state. We propose CAST (Concept-guided Artifact Suppression Tuning), an SAE-based framework for auditable clinical text classification. CAST uses Sparse Autoencoders to expose sparse, human-auditable features from intermediate Transformer activations, labels SAE latents with an LLM-assisted interpretation pipeline and ICD-10 retrieval constraints, suppresses verified artifact latents via residual subtraction during fine-tuning, and provides post-hoc per-concept attributions for auditing model decisions. On MIMIC-IV discharge-note mortality prediction, CAST improves over its corresponding fine-tuned encoder baselines and remains competitive with strong LLM baselines, while producing a feature-level audit trail of the clinical concepts that support each prediction and the artifact concepts suppressed during training.

</details>


### [206] [How Language Models Organize and Structure Moral Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27402)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过训练六个独立的线性探针（分别对应道德基础理论中的六个类别），研究大型语言模型如何在表示空间中组织和几何化道德知识。研究发现这些方向既不坍缩为单一道德检测器，也不彼此隔离，而是跨越近最大数量的独立维度并共享一个正公共分量——这是整合的签名，且相对于匹配的非道德概念具有道德特异性。这种几何结构在不同架构和规模下保持一致，在预训练早期即达到整合状态。模型对道德困境的表征不是预先解决的判断，而是道德张力本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLM能检测道德内容，但检测只是一个较低标准。作者想探究模型是否更进一步：能否区分不同道德基础类别，并在表示空间中以几何方式组织它们之间的关系。这有助于理解LLM如何组织道德知识，以及是否反映道德基础理论（MFT）的预测。

Method: 在开放权重语言模型上训练六个独立的线性探针，每个对应MFT的一个类别（care/harm, fair/cheat, lib/oppress, loy/betray, auth/subv, sanc/degrade），然后在表示空间中检验这些方向的几何关系。包括：分析维度之间的独立性、公共分量、与匹配的非道德概念基准对比、跨架构和规模的稳定性、预训练阶段的演化、与MFT个体化/约束区分的对比、以及道德困境方向的组合分解分析。

Result: 六个道德方向既不坍缩为单一检测器也不彼此隔离，而是跨越近最大数量的独立维度且共享正公共分量（道德特定性：平均成对余弦0.26 vs 非道德0.013）。几何结构跨架构和规模一致，在预训练早期达到整合状态。未发现MFT预测的个体化/约束区分，结构反映语料统计。道德困境方向部分由其组成基础组合而成（2.7倍于错配基线），大部分方差编码冲突特异性结构。

Conclusion: LLM以"整合-分化"的几何模式组织道德知识：不同道德基础在独立维度上分化，同时共享一个道德特定的公共分量实现整合。模型表示道德张力本身而非预先解决的判断，但未遵循MFT的个体化/约束理论预测，而是受语料统计驱动。

Abstract: How do large language models (LLMs) organize moral knowledge? Models detect moral content broadly, but detection is a low bar. We ask whether they go further, distinguishing moral foundations from one another and organizing the relationships between them geometrically.
  We train six independent linear probes on open-weight language models, one per Moral Foundations Theory (MFT) category (care/harm, fair/cheat, lib/oppress, loy/betray, auth/subv, sanc/degrade), and examine how the resulting directions relate to each other in representation space. We find the directions neither collapse into a single moral detector nor isolate from one another. Rather, they span a near-maximal number of independent dimensions while sharing a positive common component. The shared component is the signature of integration, and it is moral-specific relative to a matched non-moral concept battery built identically (mean pairwise cosine 0.26 vs. 0.013).
  The geometry is consistent across architectures and scale and reaches its integration regime early in pre-training, well before probe accuracy saturates. The structure the model discovers shows no evidence of the individualizing/binding distinction predicted by Moral Foundations Theory (an underpowered test: only 20 candidate partitions exist) but rather reflects corpus statistics. Extending to moral dilemmas, each dilemma direction partially composes from its component foundations, at 2.7x a mismatched-pair baseline, while the majority of its variance encodes conflict-specific structure. The model represents moral tension itself, not a pre-resolved judgment.

</details>


### [207] [Consolidating RLVR Capabilities Across Domains: A Deep Dive into Fusion Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27409)
*Siye Wu,Kai Yang,Yuchen Cai,Xin Xu,Peng-Yuan Wang,Jiaxuan Wang,Jiashun Liu,Jiafei Lyu,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本篇论文系统比较了三种用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）融合多领域大语言模型能力的范式：Merge（合并专家任务向量）、Mix RL（混合数据集训练）和多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD，同时利用数据和教师）。在共享专家和数据条件下，三种方法平均性能差异不超过1.4分，但单基准差距可达8.6分，且领域级差异与任务向量几何中的跨域关系相关。训练动力学揭示各自约束，最终给出实际选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升大语言模型的特定能力，但覆盖多能力通常需要分别训练领域专家再整合。由于Merge、Mix RL和MOPD这三种融合范式大多被孤立研究，缺乏系统性比较，导致从业者不清楚它们的相对优劣及如何选择。

Method: 在多种模型规模和跨多域基准套件上，使用共享专家和共享数据，对三种融合范式（Merge：合并专家任务向量；Mix RL：混合各领域数据集进行RL训练；MOPD：多教师在线策略蒸馏，同时复用数据和教师）进行统一对比实验，并分析训练动力学、任务向量几何与跨域关系。

Result: 三种方法平均性能差异最多1.4分，但单个基准上差距可达8.6分。领域级性能变化与任务向量几何中反映的跨域关系一致。训练动力学揭示：Mix RL依赖领域混合比例，MOPD性能受限于教师，Merge将所有专家更新压缩为一个。三者均提升单样本准确率，但未显著改善解覆盖范围，也未损失保留能力。

Conclusion: 据此给出实用选择指南：已有专家且追求低成本融合时选Merge；无专家、需训练统一模型时选Mix RL（需按跨域迁移调整领域比例）；需要保留领域特定收益、且不追求超过教师或最小化端到端成本时选MOPD。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves specific capabilities of large language models, but covering multiple capabilities often involves training separate domain experts and subsequently consolidating them. We organize three fusion paradigms by the artefacts they reuse: Merge combines expert task vectors, Mix RL pools their datasets, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) uses both. Because they have largely been studied in isolation, how they compare and how to choose among them remain unclear. We compare all three using shared experts and data across model scales and a multi-domain benchmark suite. Although their average performance differs by at most 1.4 points, the gap reaches 8.6 points on a single benchmark, with domain-level variation tracking cross-domain relations visible in task-vector geometry. Training dynamics expose distinct constraints: Mix RL depends on domain mixture proportions, MOPD remains bounded by its teachers, and Merge compresses all expert updates into one. All three improve single-sample accuracy without measurable gains in solution coverage or losses in held-out capabilities. These results yield a practical guideline: use Merge when experts already exist and cheap fusion is paramount; Mix RL when training a unified model without experts, with domain proportions adjusted for cross-domain transfer; and MOPD when preserving domain-specific gains matters more than surpassing teachers or minimizing end-to-end cost.

</details>


### [208] [Boosting LLM Exploration via Weak-Model Guidance in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27420)
*Xingyu Shen,Huishuai Zhang,Peng Li,Yinchun Wang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过注入外部小模型生成的"不熟悉前缀"来缓解RLVR训练中策略熵坍缩问题的方法，该方法利用分布差异驱动的非参数扰动，在不增加SFT或复杂奖励设计的情况下，显著扩展推理覆盖范围并提升pass@k性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR显著提升LLM推理能力的同时会导致策略熵迅速下降，造成推理覆盖范围收窄，pass@k（尤其是大k时）性能退化。现有方法多依赖算法正则化来缓解熵坍缩，但忽略了跨模型非参数扰动的潜力，缺乏从分布差异角度探索外部前缀对RLVR探索动态影响的研究。

Method: 提出"外部前缀"方法：利用一个更小、更弱的语言模型生成部分推理轨迹，将其作为前缀强制目标模型基于这些不熟悉的前缀继续生成答案。这些外部前缀通过引入分布差异，有效破坏模型的过度自信，鼓励模型探索不同的推理路径。方法不需要额外SFT、精细奖励设计或复杂提示工程。

Result: 在多个数学基准上的实验表明，该方法一致优于原生RLVR。关键发现是性能增益随k增大而愈加显著，表明推理覆盖范围得到了实质性扩展；同时有效缓解熵坍缩问题。还实证研究了外部前缀的潜力，揭示了分布差异对RLVR训练中探索动态的影响机制。

Conclusion: 通过跨模型非参数扰动（外部弱模型前缀）可以简单高效地保持RLVR训练中的生成多样性，扩展推理覆盖，且性能提升随采样规模增大而放大，为缓解RLVR熵坍缩提供了无需复杂正则化的新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly improves LLM reasoning but often causes a drop in policy entropy, leading to narrowed reasoning coverage and degraded pass@$k$ for large $k$. While existing methods mitigate this entropy collapse through algorithmic regularizations, cross-model non-parametric perturbation is also neglected. In this work, we propose a simple yet effective approach to preserve the generative diversity of LLMs during RLVR. Instead of relying solely on internal exploration, we force the target model to generate answers based on partial reasoning trajectories generated by a smaller, weaker language models. These unfamiliar prefixes effectively disrupt over-confidence and encourage the exploration of distinct reasoning paths. We empirically study the potential of outer prefixes, revealing the mechanism of the impact of distributional discrepancy to the exploration dynamics in RLVR training. Experiments across multiple mathematical benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms vanilla RLVR. Notably, the performance gain becomes increasingly pronounced as $k$ scales up, demonstrating a substantial expansion of reasoning coverage. Furthermore, our approach efficiently mitigates entropy collapse without requiring additional SFT, intricate reward designs, or complex prompting.

</details>


### [209] [Stochastic Estimation of Transduced Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27428)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Manuel de Prada Corral,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无放回抽样的束求和算法来估计转导语言模型(TLM)中目标前缀概率的无偏估计量，相比传统的阈值剪枝束求和方法，它能估算剪枝丢失的质量、节省计算量并保证以概率1停止，在文本和DNA任务上均取得了更优的精度-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转导语言模型(TLM)通过将预训练的源语言模型与功能有限状态转导器组合来对目标字符串建模，但计算目标前缀概率需要对可能指数级或无限多的源字符串求和。现有方法使用基于源前缀概率的阈值剪枝束求和逼近，只能得到误差未知的下界。

Method: 提出一种无放回重采样的方法：从源前缀中无放回地采样，并以包含概率的倒数对每个选中前缀加权。递归应用该修正即可得到目标前缀概率的无偏估计量，并能估计阈值剪枝丢失的质量。束求和算法扩展保留的源前缀并采样决定保留哪些前缀，随着运行估计中概率质量的增加而减少保留的前缀数量。

Result: 在百科全书文本和DNA数据上，与有放回重采样的序贯蒙特卡洛基线相比：文本上获得更好的计算-方差权衡，DNA上在相同最大粒子数下获得更低误差。在DNA到氨基酸转导任务上，相比阈值剪枝束求和将运行时间减少若干数量级，使长目标字符串的前缀概率估计变得可行。在已发表的阅读时间分析中，用无偏采样替换阈值剪枝大幅降低了语料惊讶度估计，但不改变已发表的结论。

Conclusion: 无放回采样加权方法为TLM中的目标前缀概率估计提供了无偏且高效的计算框架，在精度、计算效率和可扩展性上均优于现有近似方法，同时不会改变下游应用的定性结论。

Abstract: Transduced language models (TLMs) compose a pretrained \emph{source} language model with a functional finite-state transducer to induce a language model over \emph{target} strings. Computing the probability of a target prefix under a TLM amounts to summing the source-model probabilities of all source strings that the transducer maps to target strings beginning with that prefix. This set can be exponentially large or infinite. Prior work uses a computational shortcut based on source prefix probabilities, then approximates the resulting sum with threshold-pruned beam summing. This produces a lower bound with unknown error. Instead, we resample source prefixes without replacement and reweight each selected prefix by the inverse of its inclusion probability. We show that applying this correction recursively gives an unbiased estimator of the target prefix probability and lets us estimate the mass lost by threshold pruning. Our beam-summing algorithm extends the retained source prefixes and samples which prefixes to keep, reducing their number as more probability mass is added to the running estimate. This can save computation and guarantees that the run halts with probability one. We evaluate the method on encyclopedic text and DNA against sequential Monte Carlo baselines that resample with replacement. It achieves a better compute--variance tradeoff on text and lower error at the same maximum number of particles on DNA. On a DNA-to-amino-acid transduction, it reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude relative to threshold-pruned beam summing and makes estimating prefix probabilities for long target strings feasible. Replacing threshold pruning with unbiased sampling in a published reading-time analysis substantially lowers the estimated corpus surprisal but leaves the published conclusions unchanged.

</details>


### [210] [TTPO: Test-Time Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27448)
*Aozhe Wang,Zhengxi Lu,Jianze Wang,Shangke Lv,Ying Liu,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Hua Yang,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时策略优化（TTPO），一种无需真值标签的测试时训练方法。TTPO利用非对称目标：对与多数投票伪标签一致的rollout执行OPSD蒸馏，对不一致的rollout执行分组RL惩罚。通过token级选择进一步优化两个分支，即使在伪标签频繁出错时也能保持良好训练，在五个竞赛级基准上匹配了有标签监督的OPSD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练方法（如RL和OPSD）在数学推理上取得了显著进展，但依赖真值标签，无法进行测试时训练（TTT）。用多数投票伪标签替代真值是自然方案，但投票错误会污染教师模型并误导所有token。作者观察到这种失败模式是不对称的：与伪标签不一致的rollout无论投票是否正确都通常是错误的，这为设计更稳健的目标提供了机会。

Method: TTPO采用非对称优化目标：通过OPSD蒸馏与伪标签一致的rollout，通过分组RL惩罚不一致的rollout。同时引入token级选择细化两个分支——蒸馏分支对已收敛的位置降权，RL分支仅惩罚高置信度的错误。多数投票路由机制随模型提升提供更紧密的自监督信号。

Result: 在五个竞赛级基准上，TTPO在无需任何标签的情况下匹配了标签监督的OPSD性能。将Qwen3-1.7B在测试时训练中从38.0%提升至45.2%，在无思考模式下提升+25.2%至+36.4%，并展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: TTPO成功实现了无标签的测试时策略优化，通过利用投票不一致性的不对称特征，在伪标签出错时仍能保持稳定的训练信号，为大型语言模型的测试时训练提供了实用的无监督方案。

Abstract: Recent prominent post-training methods, such as Reinforcement Learning (RL) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), have driven rapid progress in mathematical reasoning for large language models, yet their reliance on ground-truth labels precludes test-time training (TTT). Replacing ground truth with majority-vote pseudo-labels is a natural alternative, yet it is fragile: an incorrect vote corrupts the teacher and misleads every token. We observe that this failure mode is asymmetric: rollouts that disagree with the pseudo-label are typically wrong regardless of whether the vote itself is correct. Building on this observation, we propose Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO), an asymmetric objective that distills agreeing rollouts via OPSD and penalizes disagreeing rollouts with Grouped RL. Token-level selection further refines both branches: distillation down-weights already-converged positions, while RL penalizes only confident errors. Both updates remain well-grounded even under frequent pseudo-label errors, and majority-vote routing yields tighter self-supervision as the model improves. Without any labels, TTPO matches label-supervised OPSD on five competition-level benchmarks, raises Qwen3-1.7B from 38.0% to 45.2% in TTT, yields +25.2% to +36.4% without thinking, and shows strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [211] [CritICL: Inference-Time Weak-to-Strong Generalization from Small Language Model Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27455)
*Yufan Wu,Yinghui He,Zhengyi Hu,Lang Wei,Ruichen Li,Qifan Yang,Ting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CritICL，一种基于弱模型失败模式来引导强模型推理的高效推理时框架，通过批评式上下文示例（CritICL-dynamic和CritICL-static两种变体）在显著降低生成次数和token开销的同时，达到与测试时扩展方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）方法显著提升了LLM的推理能力，但通常依赖重复生成或外部验证，计算成本高昂。作者观察到同一模型家族中，不同规模的模型在失败模式上呈现结构化规律，因此希望将这些"失败"转化为有价值的指导信号，而非简单丢弃，以实现高效推理增强。

Method: CritICL利用弱模型（较小规模模型）产生的失败模式，将批评（critique）融入推理时的上下文示例中。框架包含两个变体：(1) CritICL-dynamic，自适应地预测特定输入对应的失败模式并检索相关批评示例；(2) CritICL-static，使用全局失败模式画像提供稳定的指导。两者均通过批评式上下文示例（critique-based in-context examples）引导强模型在推理中规避已知陷阱。

Result: 实验表明CritICL在标准上下文学习的基础上持续取得性能提升，与测试时扩展方法相比性能具有竞争力或更优，且大幅减少了生成次数和token开销。

Conclusion: CritICL证明了"失败模式"可以作为有效的推理指导信号，通过将弱模型的系统性错误转化为批评式上下文示例，能够在不依赖昂贵重复采样或外部验证的情况下实现高质量、高效率的LLM推理增强。

Abstract: Recent advances in inference-time scaling have significantly improved the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, these methods typically rely on repeated generation or external verification. To address this limitation, we introduce CritICL, a novel inference-time framework that improves reasoning while maintaining high efficiency. Our key insight is that LLM failure modes exhibit structured patterns across model scales within the same family. Instead of treating failures as undesirable outputs, CritICL leverages them as a source of guidance. Specifically, we utilize failure modes derived from weaker models and incorporate them into inference through critique-based in-context examples. We propose two variants: CritICL-dynamic, which adaptively predicts input-specific failure modes and retrieves critiques, and CritICL-static, which uses a global failure mode profile to provide stable guidance. Experimental results show that CritICL consistently outperforms standard in-context learning and achieves performance competitive with or superior to test-time scaling methods, while requiring significantly fewer generations and lower token cost. Code available at: https://github.com/umwyf/CRITICL

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EduRiskX是一个神经符号框架，融合了基于时间变换器的预测器与F-Logic符号推理，用于在线教育中学生学业风险的早期检测与可解释预测。在OULAD数据集上达到0.900准确率和0.894 F1分数，平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率为94.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线教育学业风险预测模型存在两大痛点：早期检测能力有限，以及缺乏可解释性。这种"黑箱"困境导致模型在真实教育场景中难以被教育工作者信任和采纳，亟需一种既能保证预测性能又能提供透明决策依据的解决方案。

Method: EduRiskX采用神经-符号双模块架构：(1) 神经组件基于时间变换器，建模纵向学生活动序列，结合时间注意力、类加权损失和动态周截断技术；(2) 符号组件是基于F-Logic的规则库，根植于参与理论（Engagement Theory）和学生整合模型（Student Integration Model），仅从训练数据构建，模拟人类教育工作者的诊断逻辑；(3) 两者通过基于逻辑回归的融合机制结合，学习各信号源的相对贡献。

Result: 在OUAD数据集上采用严格的80/10/10学生级划分，EduRiskX在学期末（第38周）达到0.900准确率和0.894 F1分数，平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率94.30%。与PatchTST、iTransformer等SOTA时间序列模型及LSTM、CNN等基线相比，在相同条件下取得了更高的召回率和更早的风险识别能力。

Conclusion: EduRiskX不仅提升了预测性能，更重要的是通过F-Logic模块提供了结构化、基于规则的解释，将预测结果与可观察行为模式和教育理论相连接，解决了黑箱信任危机，为在线教育学业风险预测提供了既有高性能又具可解释性的综合解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [213] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了独立LLM与四步智能体流水线在ICU死亡风险解释中的表现。XGBoost模型在2,353例ICU住院（死亡率8.1%）上达到AUROC 0.855、AUPRC 0.332。在38例解释子集中，智能体流水线消除了独立LLM中出现的1例结局泄露，并在指南依据、数值特异性和合理性上表现更优，但SHAP对齐度和方向一致性较低，提示两者应互补使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型能准确预测ICU死亡率，但特征归因方法单独使用难以提供临床所需的叙述性解释。大型语言模型有望弥合这一差距，而多步骤智能体流水线可通过分离数据解读、指南核查和最终解释三个环节来扩展这一能力。本研究旨在保留独立与智能体对比框架的同时，更明确地呈现主要临床发现。

Method: 使用eICU Demo本地数据集的2,353例ICU住院记录（死亡率8.1%），以XGBoost构建预测模型。随后在一个分层抽样的38例解释子集上，比较独立LLM与四步智能体流水线生成的解释质量。评估指标包括结局泄露检测、SHAP对齐度（Jaccard）、方向一致性、指南依据、数值特异性及临床合理性，并在与SHAP评审子集重叠的14例上进行详细对比。

Result: XGBoost在预测方面达到AUROC 0.855（95% CI 0.796-0.906）和AUPRC 0.332（95% CI 0.217-0.494）。独立LLM产生1例明确结局泄露，而智能体流水线为零。在14例重叠案例中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐度更高（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）、方向一致性更高（92.9% vs 78.6%），但智能体流水线在指南依据（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和合理性（0.700 vs 0.671）上更优。

Conclusion: 智能体分解可能提升与安全相关的指南依据和患者特异细节，但在高风险风险解释场景下，应配合基于特征归因的核查机制联合使用，以弥补其在SHAP对齐和方向一致性上的不足。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [214] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个关于大型模型（LMs）在电池预测与健康管理（BPHM）中应用的全面综述，系统梳理了基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练的大型模型如何克服传统BPHM方法在计算效率、跨域泛化、数据依赖和可解释性方面的瓶颈，并提出了面向未来研究的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法（基于物理模型和任务中心深度学习）存在计算效率低、参数化困难、跨域泛化能力不足、依赖大量标注的run-to-failure数据以及模型可解释性差等问题。大型模型的出现为解决这些长期性瓶颈提供了变革性的新范式，但目前缺乏系统性的综述来整合和评估这一领域的进展。

Method: 该综述首先阐明支持大型模型的基础技术，包括Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集和参数高效微调（PEFT）技术；然后从四个关键维度对近期进展进行分类：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、整合领域知识实现可解释性、以及支持系统级自动化。

Result: 综述指出大型模型在BPHM领域已展现出有前景的结果，但仍然面临数据可访问性、智能验证、可信赖性和部署可行性等方面的重大挑战。为此提出了包括构建协作数据生态系统、针对工业应用验证智能、通过物理信息设计增强可信赖性以及实现高效端侧部署在内的研究路线图。

Conclusion: 本文建立了理解和推进LM驱动的BPHM的系统性方法，为研究人员和实践者开发下一代电池管理系统提供了关键见解，以实现电池全生命周期的安全、可靠和自主运行。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [215] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PICasso是一个AI辅助框架，能够从自然语言规格说明自动合成、验证和优化光子集成电路（PIC）。通过结构化NL->YAML->GDS生成管线、PDK知识注入、自动布局布线、DRC/LVS验证和SAX光子仿真，PICasso显著提升了LLM生成的满足度（结构Spec@3最高92.7%，功能Spec@3最高52%），并将插入损耗均值从4.98 dB降至3.25 dB。同时引入PIC-Set基准，包含36个参数化PIC设计任务和统一评估协议，证明结构化领域约束+物理验证+仿真反馈能将LLM从脆弱的网表生成器转变为实用PIC设计智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子集成电路设计高度复杂且依赖专业领域知识，传统的基于LLM的直接生成方法缺乏结构约束、物理验证和仿真反馈，难以产生可制造且性能优化的设计。现有的评估基准也缺乏系统性的指标来衡量AI驱动光子设计的质量。

Method: PICasso采用结构化NL->YAML->GDS三级生成管线，结合PDK感知的知识注入、自动布局布线（P&R）、DRC/LVS物理验证以及基于SAX的光子仿真。同时构建PIC-Set基准（36个参数化任务），引入新的评估指标：结构/功能Spec@k、优化效率和扰动鲁棒性，并实现仿真引导的迭代优化来降低插入损耗。

Result: 与vanilla LLM生成相比，PICasso显著提升端到端规格满足度。在高复杂度电路上，结构Spec@3达到92.7%，功能Spec@3达到52%。优化后电路平均插入损耗从4.98 dB降至3.25 dB（提升1.74 dB），同时保持与手动GUI流程可比的计算耗时。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈的结合，是使LLM从脆弱的网表生成器转变为实用PIC设计智能体的关键。PICasso证明了AI辅助设计能够产出可制造的布局，并在性能优化上超越人工流程，为AI驱动的光子集成电路设计建立了新的基准和评估范式。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [216] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFQA（计算密集型金融问答）框架，一种确定性工具接地多智能体LLM框架，将语言理解与数值执行分离，通过专用智能体处理查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，同时用确定性Python工具执行金融计算。在固定存款问答基准上达到95.54%计算密集型查询准确率和90.87%整体准确率，显著优于直接LLM基线，且17B开源大模型在此框架下优于更大的前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自然语言任务上表现强劲，但在多步金融计算中经常产生数值错误但看似合理的答案。计算密集型金融问答需要对结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和基于规则的约束进行精确推理，直接使用LLM难以保证数值可靠性，因此需要一种将语言理解与数值执行分离的架构性解决方案。

Method: CIFQA采用多智能体架构，将任务分解为五个专业化智能体：查询解释（query interpretation）、路由（routing）、参数提取（parameter extraction）、计算规划（computation planning）和响应生成（response generation）。语言理解任务由LLM智能体负责，而所有金融计算和规则应用由确定性Python工具执行，确保数值结果的精确性。该方法在固定存款问答场景中实例化，包括精确利率查找、存期计算、滚动年度调整和提前支取逻辑等确定性组件。

Result: CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%准确率，整体准确率90.87%，显著优于在提供完整公式、利率表和基准指令情况下直接使用LLM基线的表现。消融实验表明确定性组件（精确利率查找、存期计算、滚动年度调整、提前支取逻辑）是性能的关键贡献者。17B开源模型在CIFQA框架内超过使用相同金融信息的更大规模前沿模型，表明架构设计比模型规模对数值可靠性更重要。

Conclusion: CIFQA通过模块化架构将语言理解与数值执行分离，有效解决了LLM在计算密集型金融问答中的数值不精确问题。该框架虽在固定存款查询上评估，但具有泛化到其他计算密集型金融推理任务的潜力。核心洞见是：在金融计算场景中，架构设计（确定性工具与LLM智能体协同）比模型规模更能决定数值可靠性。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [217] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARL框架，将自导强化学习（autotelic RL）应用于Lenia连续元胞自动机，实现闭环控制复杂系统。CARL通过目标条件策略以最小局部扰动干预系统，能发现稳定孤子、操控已有孤子运动方向，并支持人类通过高层指令实时引导孤子穿越迷宫，且策略可零样本泛化到分布外条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索元胞自动机等复杂系统的方法多为开环模式：仅设定初始条件、执行完整模拟、观察结果，无法在执行过程中干预。这种范式限制了研究者对涌现现象的主动控制与系统化探索能力，亟需一种闭环、可干预的框架。

Method: 方法核心是闭环自导强化学习框架：智能体自主采样多样化目标，学习一个目标条件策略（goal-conditioned policy），通过最小化、局部化的扰动对复杂系统进行干预。在Lenia（连续元胞自动机）上实例化为CARL系统，训练覆盖多样目标、更新规则和随机初始状态。

Result: 三项核心能力验证：(1) CARL在多种Lenia更新规则下发现稳定孤子的成功率高于启发式基线；(2) 通过少量干预学会操控已有孤子的运动方向，证明不仅能创造自组织模式还能控制它们；(3) 人类可实时通过高层方向指令引导孤子穿过迷宫，智能体将指令转化为低层干预。训练后的策略零样本泛化到多种分布外条件。

Conclusion: 结果表明了一条通向人工实验智能体（artificial experimentalist agents）的路径：这类智能体能够在自主或人类引导下，于复杂系统中发现并控制涌现现象，为复杂系统的系统性探索与操控提供新范式。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [218] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了能量预测中的"精度-效率悖论"：高精度的能量预测模型可能因其推理能耗和电池老化反而导致净能量亏损。作者提出一个总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗和电池老化统一视为能量损失形式，以最小化净能量损失为目标进行能量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能量预测研究只追求预测精度最大化，忽视了边缘AI推理本身消耗的能量以及电池老化带来的能量容量下降，导致在军事等关键边缘环境下出现"预测越准、净能量越亏"的悖论。

Method: 提出总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一建模为能量损失。该框架在热敏感边缘环境中评估复杂架构的精度收益与高运行强度带来的总能量损失之间的平衡。

Result: 实验表明，在热敏感边缘环境中，复杂架构因精度提升所节省的能量，往往被其高运行强度导致的总能量损失所抵消甚至超越。

Conclusion: 能量预测模型的设计不应仅以精度为导向，而应基于TCO框架在精度与能耗（含推理能耗和电池老化）之间寻求最优平衡，以实现净能量损失最小化。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>


### [219] [LLMs for Academic Workflows: An Evaluation of Literature Reviews Generated with Short and Long Context Windows of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26145)
*Muhammad Ali Chaudhry,Xinyuan Hao,Haifa Alwahaby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了LLM在短上下文和长上下文设置下生成的20篇文献综述，由两名研究员在15个维度上进行评价。研究发现AI生成的文献综述需要人工监督才能达到学术出版标准；上下文窗口增大虽能整合更广泛信息并保持连贯性，但会加剧内容重复、遗漏关键工作和重描述轻综合等问题。AI综述可提供基础性概述，但必须经过领域专家批判性评估和打磨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究上下文窗口大小对AI生成文献综述质量的影响，以及AI在支撑文献综述写作中的角色，以了解LLMs在学术写作辅助中的能力和局限性。

Method: 从Semantic Scholar和Arxiv获取研究文献源，生成20篇AI文献综述，分别置于短上下文和长上下文设置下，由两名研究者从15个维度进行评估。

Result: AI生成的文献综述需要人工监督才能满足学术出版标准。上下文窗口增加时，LLMs能整合更广泛信息并保持长输入的连贯性，但同时加剧了内容重复、关键工作遗漏以及重描述轻综合的问题。

Conclusion: AI生成的综述可作为基础性概述，但其输出必须由领域专家批判性评估和精炼。未来研究应考虑集成其他LLMs和不同领域的微调模型，采用人类专业知识与AI能力相结合的混合方法来解决本研究的局限性。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [220] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系超图变换器（RHT），一种将关系数据库表示为超图并学习五维嵌入（PentE）的统一架构。该架构采用稀疏关系注意力机制，其复杂度与平均关系度成比例而非实体数量的平方。在Synthea合成电子健康记录数据集上，RHT在SNOMED CT条件代码的多标签预测任务中展现了最强的嵌入语义连贯性和计算可扩展性，而XGBoost在罕见代码召回率上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习在医疗保健和复杂信息系统中仍是一个重大挑战。关系数据融合了多种复杂性来源：大规模数据量、高维变量、高基数的类别特征、复杂的表间依赖以及重复的时间观测。现有方法难以同时处理这些复杂性，需要一种统一架构来高效建模关系数据。

Method: RHT方法包括三个核心组件：(1) 将关系数据库表示为超图结构，捕获多表之间的高阶关系；(2) 学习五维嵌入（PentE）来编码实体及关系的丰富语义；(3) 采用稀疏关系注意力机制，其计算复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量的平方，从而保证可扩展性。作者还提供了完整的开源参考实现。

Result: 在Synthea合成EHR数据集上的多标签SNOMED CT条件代码预测任务中：XGBoost在罕见代码召回率上达到最高；RHT在嵌入语义连贯性上表现最强；RHT保持了计算可扩展性。消融研究量化了各架构组件的贡献。基线对比包括表格、关系和图（含时序）方法。

Conclusion: RHT在嵌入语义质量上显著优于基线，但在极端长尾标签分布下的罕见类别召回率仍不及梯度提升树方法，提示未来可探索结合两者优势的混合策略。MIMIC-IV上的临床验证已纳入计划。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [221] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个LLM流水线，用于从536篇同行评审的基于代理建模论文中提取模型相关信息，并与人工系统性文献综述的结果进行对比。GPT-4.1的论文级准确率约为77.95%，GPT-5.0约为81.67%，字段级准确率在32.40%至100%之间。研究发现LLM之间的一致性可以作为输出质量的潜在指标：低一致性可能暗示幻觉，而高一致性伴随低准确率可能指向人工数据集的噪声或错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的最新进展为简化和自动化研究流程（包括系统性文献综述）创造了新机会，但LLM在SLR中的实际表现和可靠性缺乏系统性验证，需要实证研究来评估其在建模与仿真领域大规模文献综述中的潜力与局限性。

Method: 构建了一个LLM流水线，用于从536篇同行评审的Agent-Based Modeling论文中自动提取模型相关信息；将GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0的结果与人工完成的系统性文献综述进行对比；通过论文级和字段级两个维度的准确率评估性能，并分析LLM之间的一致性与其输出质量之间的关联。

Result: 论文级准确率方面，GPT-4.1约为77.95%，GPT-5.0约为81.67%；字段级准确率在32.40%到100.00%之间波动，复杂或主观性较强的字段表现不够可靠。LLM之间的一致性可作为质量信号：低一致性可能表明幻觉，而高一致性结合低准确率则可能指向人工数据集中的噪声或错误。

Conclusion: 研究为LLM在SLR中的提示词开发提供了实践见解，同时揭示了使用LLM进行建模与仿真领域全规模系统性文献综述的潜力与局限性，并提出了利用LLM间一致性作为输出质量指示器的新视角。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [222] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了电信客户流失预测的模型可解释性问题。研究在IBM Telco基准数据集上基准测试了四种分类器（Logistic回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM），发现它们性能几乎持平（AUC差距仅0.011），并引入全局及实例级SHAP/LIME可解释性分析，识别出在网时长、总费用和月付合同为关键流失信号。进一步提出四层CRM集成架构，将风险评分和归因向量转化为分层细分、结构化留客行动模板及再训练反馈循环，预计可降低3.3-5.3个百分点的整体流失率，每活动周期节省约19.9万-31.9万美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信运营商面临高额且持续的客户流失问题（成熟市场月流失率约1.9%，年损失数十亿美元）。虽然现有预测模型能准确识别高风险客户，但高性能的集成和非线性模型往往缺乏透明度，前端CRM流失专家无法仅凭概率分数设计个性化干预方案。论文旨在弥合"高预测精度"与"可操作解释"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在IBM Telco客户流失基准数据集（7,043条记录、19个特征、26.5%流失率，训练集经SMOTE平衡至50%）上基准测试四种分类器：Logistic回归、随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM，采用5折交叉验证。可解释性分析分两个粒度：全局SHAP排序确定重要流失信号，实例级SHAP和LIME分解揭示单个预测的驱动因素。在此基础上提出四层CRM集成架构：将风险评分和归因向量转化为分层细分、将顶级特征映射到结构化留客行动模板、并将活动结果路由至再训练反馈循环。

Result: Logistic回归取得最强AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM取得最高准确率（78.42%）；四种模型AUC差距在0.011以内（0.831-0.841），5折交叉验证表明领先模型实际表现持平。全局SHAP识别出在网时长、总费用和月付合同为最主导的流失信号。针对最高风险五分位客户的干预预计可降低整体流失率3.3-5.3个百分点，每活动周期可节省约19.9万-31.9万美元的估计损失。

Conclusion: 论文证明高性能流失预测模型可以通过结构化可解释性分析与运营架构设计，无缝嵌入前端CRM工作流。即使在模型性能几乎持平的情况下，可解释性差异（而非纯粹精度）应成为模型选型的核心标准。该四层CRM集成框架为电信行业提供了从"预测"到"行动"的完整闭环路径，同时通过反馈循环实现模型持续优化。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>


### [223] [EEG-to-Report: An Annotation and Feature-Text Framework for Training Language Models on Clinical EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26153)
*Xuan-The Tran,Le Trung Kien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EEG-to-Report，一个基于浏览器的EEG标注与特征文本框架，将常规EEG阅片与AI-ready数据集构建相结合。框架集成了多格式EEG导入、通道标准化、交互式查看器，以及结合键入文本和转录语音笔记的多模态标注层，为每个标注片段计算标准化的频谱、时域、熵、Hjorth、连通性和棘波相关特征，并与临床描述一起存储在可移植的JSON模式中，形成对齐的特征-文本对。框架还包含自动报告模块，将卷积网络集成与大语言模型耦合，为神经科医生审阅生成临床叙述草稿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG报告仍然主要依赖手工和耗时的过程，而现有的EEG软件生态系统无法生成训练现代语言模型所需的结构化EEG-文本监督信号。大多数工具包侧重于可视化或预处理，对生成高质量AI训练数据集的工作流支持有限，亟需一个能连接常规EEG阅片与AI数据集构建的框架。

Method: 开发EEG-to-Report浏览器框架，包含：（1）多格式EEG数据导入与通道标准化；（2）交互式查看器与多模态标注层（键入文本+转录语音笔记）；（3）特征提取引擎，计算频谱、时域、熵、Hjorth、连通性和棘波相关描述符；（4）可移植JSON模式存储特征与临床描述的配对；（5）自动报告模块，使用卷积网络集成结合大语言模型生成临床叙述草稿供神经科医生审阅。

Result: 通过试点标注，验证了EEG-to-Report能够简化标注工作流并生成可编辑的草稿报告，为自动化EEG报告系统提供了可复用的基础。

Conclusion: EEG-to-Report提供了一种将常规EEG临床阅片与多模态AI数据集构建相结合的可复用框架，填补了现有工具在生成结构化EEG-文本监督数据方面的空白，为自动化EEG报告系统的开发奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) reporting remains largely manual and time-consuming, and current EEG software ecosystems do not produce the structured EEG-text supervision needed for training modern language models. Most toolboxes focus on visualization or preprocessing, providing limited support for workflows that generate high-quality datasets for AI. We introduce EEG-to-Report, a browser-based annotation and feature-text framework that links routine EEG review with the construction of AI-ready datasets. The framework integrates multi-format EEG ingestion, channel standardization, and an interactive viewer with a multimodal annotation layer that combines typed text and transcribed voice notes. For each annotated segment, a feature extraction engine computes a standardized set of spectral, temporal, entropy, Hjorth, connectivity, and spike-related descriptors, stored alongside clinical descriptions in a portable JSON schema. This yields aligned feature-text pairs designed to supervise multimodal EEG-language models. The framework also includes an auto-report module that couples an ensemble of convolutional networks with a large language model to draft clinical narratives for neurologist review. Using pilot annotations, we describe how EEG-to-Report streamlines annotation workflows and produces editable draft reports, providing a reusable foundation for automated EEG reporting systems.

</details>


### [224] [Selection Bias Correction in Retail Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26156)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过400次蒙特卡洛模拟，在四种数据生成场景下比较了逆概率加权（IPW）与分层法在零售长尾分布中校正通胀估计选择偏差的表现。分层法在三个场景中优于IPW（中位误差低于0.04个百分点），而带样条倾向模型的IPW仅在平滑多项式场景下胜出。关键发现是，即使具有完美结构知识的oracle IPW在阶梯函数场景下误差仍达6.06pp（vs. 分层法的0.008pp），这归因于正性假设的严重违反而非方法本身缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售情报通常仅监控高销量热销产品，忽略了大量利基商品的"长尾"，可能导致经济指标（如通胀估计）产生选择偏差。填平这一方法论空白对准确测量真实通胀至关重要。

Method: 采用400次蒙特卡洛重复模拟，涵盖四种数据生成过程：对齐阶梯函数、平滑梯度、错位断点及多项式关系。将五种规格的逆概率加权（IPW）方法（含oracle完美规格）与不同层数的分层方法进行系统比较，评估其在零售长尾情景下的稳健性。

Result: 分层法在四个场景中的三个中表现更优，即便断点故意与人口断点错位时，中位误差仍低于0.04pp（比IPW优势达116倍）。IPW仅在平滑多项式关系下表现更好（0.007pp vs. 0.013pp）。oracle IPW在阶梯函数场景下误差为6.06pp，远高于分层法的0.008pp，原因是选择概率差异悬殊（90% vs. 1%）导致正性假设严重违反。

Conclusion: 在零售长尾分布且存在严重正性违反的情况下，分层法是更安全的工程选择。IPW的性能高度依赖场景，其失效源于因果推断基本假设（正性假设）的违反，而非方法本身的方法论劣势。研究强调了方法选择需结合具体数据特征进行上下文相关判断。

Abstract: Retail intelligence often relies on monitoring popular, high-velocity products, potentially biasing economic indicators by ignoring the "long tail" of niche items. This simulation study investigates selection bias in inflation estimation and compares correction methods across diverse data-generating processes. Through 400 Monte Carlo replications spanning four scenarios--aligned step functions, smooth gradients, misaligned breaks, and polynomial relationships--we test the robustness of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) with five specifications against stratification with varying strata counts. Our findings reveal fundamental limits of weighting methods in retail long-tail contexts: stratification achieves superior performance in three of four scenarios, maintaining sub-0.04pp median error even when boundaries deliberately misalign with population breaks (116x advantage over IPW). However, IPW with spline propensity models wins under smooth polynomial relationships (median error 0.007pp vs. 0.013pp), demonstrating context-dependency. Critically, even an oracle IPW specification with perfect structural knowledge achieves 6.06pp error compared to stratification's 0.008pp in step-function scenarios. This reflects violation of the Positivity Assumption--a fundamental causal inference requirement--rather than IPW methodological inferiority. When selection probabilities differ dramatically (90% vs. 1%), weighting methods operate outside their theoretical design envelope. These results demonstrate that stratification provides a safer engineering choice in retail long-tail distributions with severe positivity violations.

</details>


### [225] [GROUND: Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-Based Enterprise Analytics Through Governed Semantic Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26157)
*Aravind Sasidharan Pillai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROUND框架，通过治理语义层（批准的指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器和行级安全）约束LLM生成的文本到SQL查询，在生成后执行前对SQL进行schema、指标、连接、粒度、过滤、安全和成本规则验证，违规时重试或放弃。在100题合成企业报告基准上，GROUND是唯一在六个类目中零测量幻觉的系统，且在真实NHTSA数据和四个模型的对抗测试中，强制执行保证（尤其过滤和行级安全）在所有模型上零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据仓库的自然语言分析越来越重要，但生产环境受限于幻觉指标、无效连接、错误粒度、不安全数据访问和不支持的解释。现有文本到SQL系统通常基于数据库schema或检索文档进行生成，但企业报告还要求受治理的业务语义（批准的指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器、行级安全），这些需求未被现有系统满足。

Method: GROUND包含三个核心组件：(1) 提供经批准的语义定义（指标、维度、连接路径等）；(2) 将用户意图绑定到治理的指标和维度上；(3) 在SQL执行前根据schema、指标、连接、粒度、过滤、安全和成本规则验证生成的SQL，违规时重试或放弃。

Result: 在100题合成企业报告基准中，GROUND是唯一在所有六个评测类别中无测量幻觉的系统，而无治理的体系在多题上违反行级安全。仅含精确指标定义但无访问策略的语义条件仍泄露数据，表明治理不能仅靠指标保真度替代。在真实U.S. NHTSA车辆安全数据和跨四个模型三个提供商的对抗测试上，GROUND的强制保证（尤其过滤器和行级安全）在所有模型上零违规。

Conclusion: GROUND证明强制性的治理语义层能有效保证LLM生成的SQL在结构、语义和安全维度上的可靠性。强制性保证（如过滤和行级安全）在所有模型上稳健成立，但依赖判断的行为（如拒绝未定义的指标）仍存在失误空间，说明治理框架应优先强制执行可验证的规则。

Abstract: Natural-language analytics over enterprise data warehouses is increasingly important, but production use is limited by hallucinated metrics, invalid joins, wrong grain, unsafe data access, and unsupported explanations. Existing text-to-SQL systems often ground generation in database schemas or retrieved documentation, while enterprise reporting also requires governed business semantics: approved metrics, dimensions, join paths, filters, and row-level security. This paper introduces GROUND, Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions, a framework that constrains LLM-generated analytics to a governed semantic layer. GROUND supplies approved definitions, binds user intent to governed metrics and dimensions, and validates generated SQL against schema, metric, join, grain, filter, security, and cost rules before execution. On violations, it retries or abstains.
  In a 100-question synthetic enterprise-reporting benchmark, GROUND is compared with direct schema-only text-to-SQL, schema-RAG, and semantic-only grounding under one shared model. GROUND is the only system free of measured hallucinations across all six evaluated categories, while ungoverned systems violate row-level security on many questions. A semantic-only condition with exact metric definitions but no access policy still leaks data, showing that governance cannot be replaced by metric fidelity alone. The findings are replicated on real U.S. NHTSA vehicle-safety data with independent hand-authored gold and tested on an adversarial set across four models from three providers. GROUND's enforced guarantees, especially filters and row-level security, hold with zero violations on every model, while judgment-dependent behaviors such as refusing undefined metrics remain fallible.

</details>


### [226] [SAREF-based Ontology for Distributed AI Workflows across the Edge-Fog-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26160)
*Viorica Rozina Chifu,Tudor Cioara,Vasile Ofrim,Liana Toderean,Ionut Anghel,Laura Daniele,Cornelis Bouter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个符合SAREF标准的本体，用于在边-雾-云连续体中表示分布式AI工作流。通过扩展SAREF4SYST本体来建模AI管道、可执行AI作业、计算资源、部署约束和通信关系，实现了分布式AI应用的语义互操作性、自动化推理和资源感知编排。评估显示部署成功率达90-100%，平均编排决策时间低于80ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义模型对跨异构边缘、雾和云环境的分布式AI工作流表示支持有限，AI流程和资源经常使用不兼容的语义表示，影响了互操作性、编排和重用。

Method: 提出一个SAREF兼容的本体，扩展SAREF4SYST本体，引入AI管道、可执行AI作业、计算资源、部署约束和通信关系等概念，为AI工作流和异构计算基础设施提供统一语义模型。使用概念验证的智能电网能源服务编排场景进行评估，并通过能力问题（CQs）使用SPARQL查询和语义推理进行验证。

Result: 所有能力问题均通过SPARQL查询和语义推理成功验证；实验结果表明跨异构边-雾-云环境的部署成功率达90-100%，平均编排决策时间低于80ms。

Conclusion: 所提出的本体有效确保分布式AI编排的语义互操作性，支持AI工作流部署、执行推理和跨异构环境的负载适配，同时与ETSI SAREF生态系统保持完全一致。

Abstract: Nowadays semantic models provide limited support for representing distributed AI workflows and their execution across heterogeneous edge, fog, and cloud environments. Therefore, AI processes and resources are often described using incompatible semantic representations, affecting the interoperability, orchestration, and reuse. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a SAREF-compliant ontology for representing distributed AI workflows across the edge-fog-cloud continuum. We extend the SAREF4SYST ontology with concepts for modeling AI pipelines, executable AI jobs, computational resources, deployment constraints, and communication relationships, providing a unified semantic model of both AI workflows and heterogeneous computing infrastructures. The ontology enables semantic interoperability, automated reasoning, and resource-aware orchestration of distributed AI applications while remaining fully aligned with the ETSI SAREF ecosystem. The ontology is evaluated using proof-of-concept smart grid energy services orchestration scenarios and validated using competency questions showing its ability to support AI workflow deployment, execution reasoning, and workload adaptation across heterogeneous edge, fog, and cloud environments. All competency questions were successfully validated using SPARQL querying and semantic reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate deployment success rates of 90-100% with average orchestration decision times below 80 ms across heterogeneous edge-fog-cloud environments, highlighting its effectiveness on ensuring semantic interoperability for distributed AI orchestration.

</details>


### [227] [A Safety-Gated Multimodal AI Backend for Mental-Health Support: Hierarchical State Representation, Conservative Risk Fusion, and Controlled Generation in Anian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26162)
*Lei Wang,Xiao Wang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Anian，一个面向围产期心理健康支持与正念干预路由的安全门控多模态AI后端系统。该系统通过模块化流水线将生成式AI置于结构化状态表征、保守风险融合和响应门控之后，在中等或高风险时将普通AI回复替换为固定安全内容和人工支持提示。原型评估在约85.8万条标准化记录上取得L1情绪分类Micro-F1 0.9604、L2心理社会构念0.9144、L4路由0.9742，以及安全压力测试中L3高风险召回率1.0000，但作者明确指出结果仅支持内部可行性而非临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的心理健康支持系统必须区分"何时适合进行支持性对话"与"何时应阻断自由文本生成"。围产期人群心理健康脆弱，通用生成式AI可能产生不安全响应，因此需要一种结构化的安全门控机制来保障干预的安全性。

Method: Anian采用模块化流水线：用户文本或ASR语音转录映射为四层链接结构（L1情绪状态、L2心理社会构念、L3安全风险、L4干预路由）；本地文本和规则安全证据与外部语音证据通过最高风险优先规则S_fusion=max(S_local, S_external)融合；中等或高风险时阻断普通生成响应和TTS输出，替换为固定安全内容及人工支持提示。评估基于约858,295条弱标签标准化记录和233条压力测试样本。

Result: 原型评估结果显示：L1情绪分类Micro-F1为0.9604，L2心理社会构念分类为0.9144，L4路由为0.9742；L3规则引擎在233个样本的安全压力测试中高风险召回率达1.0000。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持标签框架和门控逻辑的内部可行性，但作者明确声明不构成临床有效性、诊断准确性、真实世界安全性或有效性的证据。文章报告了架构、本体论、安全融合机制、原型评估、错误分析计划和专家评审及真实世界验证路线图。

Abstract: Safety-critical mental-health support systems must distinguish when supportive conversation is appropriate from when free-form generation should be blocked. This paper presents Anian, a safety-gated multimodal AI backend for perinatal mental-health support and mindfulness-intervention routing. Anian is not intended to diagnose psychiatric conditions or replace clinical care or crisis intervention. Its modular pipeline places generative AI downstream of structured state representation, conservative risk fusion, and response gating. User text or voice-derived ASR transcripts are mapped into four linked layers: L1 emotion states, L2 psychosocial constructs, L3 safety risk, and L4 intervention routes. Local text- and rule-based safety evidence is fused with external voice-derived evidence using a highest-risk-priority rule, S_fusion = max(S_local, S_external). At moderate or high fused risk, ordinary AI-generated responses and text-to-speech delivery are blocked and replaced by fixed safety content and prompts for human support. An internal prototype evaluation used approximately 858,295 normalized records from public emotion, dialogue, mental-health-related, and Chinese dialogue corpora within a weak-label and rule-derived framework. Micro-F1 scores were 0.9604 for L1 emotion classification, 0.9144 for L2 psychosocial constructs, and 0.9742 for L4 routing. In a controlled safety stress test of 233 samples, the L3 rule engine achieved high-risk recall of 1.0000 within predefined scenarios. These findings support the internal feasibility of the label framework and gating logic but do not establish clinical validity, diagnostic accuracy, real-world safety, or effectiveness. We report the architecture, ontology, safety-fusion mechanism, prototype evaluation, error-analysis plan, and roadmap for expert-reviewed and real-world validation.

</details>


### [228] [A Task-Centric Ontology and Deterministic Domain Rules as a Verifiable Core for AI-Assisted Chemistry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26164)
*Ibrokhimsho Abduchaborov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemOntoRule，一个用于AI辅助中学化学问题求解的符号推理核心（概念验证）。其核心设计是任务中心的本体工程：围绕特定化学问题集合构建本体（JSON与RDF/Turtle序列化），结合确定性Python规则（电子结构、周期趋势、氧化态、氧化物与氢化物行为等），并辅以专家编码的fallback处理尚未规则化的问题族。系统在300道人工编写并验证的化学题上匹配了296道参考答案（98.67%）；本体驱动规则子集覆盖269题匹配266题（98.88%），fallback处理31题匹配30题。作者明确指出，因构建与评估使用同一题集，结果衡量的是实现覆盖率和内部一致性而非独立泛化，并分析了四个不匹配案例，区分了结构验证与化学正确性，提出了未来以LLM为主要将用户语言翻译为标准化本体任务框架的架构。Token效率仅为可检验的假设而非当前结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能够解释自然语言化学问题，但其内部推理难以检查、约束和验证。作者试图通过符号化、可检查的推理机制来弥补这一缺陷，为学校级化学问题求解提供可验证的AI辅助系统。

Method: 采用任务中心本体工程方法，围绕特定问题集构建轻量级本体（JSON和RDF/Turtle），结合确定性Python规则处理电子结构、周期趋势、氧化态等模式；对未覆盖的问题族采用专家编码的fallback；未来架构设想LLM作为用户语言到规范化本体任务框架的翻译器。

Result: 完整系统在300道题中匹配296道（98.67%）；本体规则子集覆盖269题匹配266题（98.88%）；fallback处理31题匹配30题；对4个不匹配案例进行了分析。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了任务中心本体在中学化学问题求解中的可行性。作者强调当前结果是实现覆盖率和内部一致性而非独立泛化的度量，并提出了未来以LLM作为翻译器的架构方向，Token效率作为可检验假设留待未来对照研究。

Abstract: Large language models can interpret natural-language chemistry questions, but their internal reasoning is difficult to inspect, constrain, and validate. This paper presents ChemOntoRule, a proof-of-concept symbolic core for AI-assisted school-level chemistry problem solving. The central design choice is task-centric ontology engineering: the ontology is constructed around the concepts, properties, relations, and executable procedures required by a defined collection of chemistry problems, rather than as a universal representation of chemistry. The implemented artifact combines a lightweight ontology serialized in JSON and RDF/Turtle with deterministic Python rules for electronic structure, periodic trends, oxidation states, oxide and hydride behavior, and related school-level reasoning patterns. A separate expert-coded fallback handles problem families not yet represented by general rules. The system was examined on 300 human-authored and manually validated chemistry problems. The complete system matched 296 of 300 reference answers (98.67%). The ontology-driven rule subset covered 269 problems and matched 266 references (98.88%); 31 problems were handled by task-specific expert-coded fallbacks, with 30 matches. Because the same collection informed ontology construction and evaluation, these results measure implemented coverage and internal consistency, not independent generalization. We analyze the four mismatches, distinguish structural validation from chemical correctness, and define a future architecture in which a language model acts primarily as a translator from user language into a normalized ontological task frame. Token efficiency is presented as a testable hypothesis for future controlled studies, not as a result of the current work.

</details>


### [229] [Refusal Is Not Robustness: Auditing Confident Fabrication in Large Language Models on a Provably Uninformative Clinical Pain Speech Transcript](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26167)
*Sagnik De,Sreenija Pavuluri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了7个大语言模型在TAME Pain语音语料库上的幻觉与弃权行为。在无信号（无痛文本）语料中，疼痛可从声学特征恢复但无法从文本中恢复，因此任何仅基于文本的疼痛评分都缺乏证据支持。在合作提示下，6个模型几乎全部弃权，并能在控制任务中正确提取疼痛评分；但在权威框架提示下，弃权行为变得依赖提示词。多数模型在被强制回答时给出低置信度估计，而Gemini 2.5 Flash和Llama 3.1 8B则持续生成高置信度的疼痛评分，虚构率分别达0.53和0.76。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉与弃权基准很少能证明模型"本不可能知道正确答案"，这使得区分合理弃权与无依据预测变得困难。本研究旨在通过设计一个模型在转录层面确实无法获得正确信息的实验协议，来严格区分这两者。

Method: 使用TAME Pain语音语料库：参与者在冷/温水浸泡下手读哈佛句子并周期性报告疼痛评分，生成5,750条无痛文本的"无信号"语音转录和1,294条明确说出疼痛评分的"信号"语句。通过对比声学特征与转录特征预测疼痛的AUC，确认转录不含疼痛信息后，在合作提示和权威框架提示两种条件下评估7个大语言模型的弃权、校准错误、置信度估计和强制回答时的虚构率。

Result: 声学特征预测疼痛AUC为0.622，而转录预测接近随机（AUC 0.489）。合作提示下，6个模型几乎全部弃权并正确执行阳性对照任务（准确率0.939-1.00），校准误差≤0.100。权威框架下弃权率受提示措辞显著影响（同一模型从0.18到1.00不等）。Gemini 2.5 Flash和Llama 3.1 8B的自信虚构率为0.53和0.76，远高于其他模型（≤0.15）。强迫回答无显著人口统计效应（所有p≥0.20）。

Conclusion: 合作提示能引导大多数模型在证据不足时恰当弃权，而权威框架提示会破坏这一能力并引入提示依赖性。部分模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash、Llama 3.1 8B）即使缺乏证据仍会生成高置信度预测，构成高风险幻觉行为。研究提供了区分合理弃权与无依据预测的实验范式。

Abstract: Hallucination and abstention benchmarks rarely establish that a model could not have known the correct answer, making it difficult to distinguish appropriate abstention from an unsupported prediction. Seven large language models were evaluated on the TAME Pain speech corpus. Participants read phonetically balanced Harvard Sentences while one hand was immersed in cold or warm water and reported pain only during periodic pain statements. This protocol generated 5,750 no signal Harvard Sentence utterances whose transcripts contained no lexical pain information and 1,294 signal pain statement utterances in which the pain rating was explicitly spoken. In the no signal arm, pain was recoverable from acoustic features (AUC 0.622, 95% CI 0.553 to 0.662), whereas transcript based prediction was near chance (AUC 0.489, 95% CI 0.418 to 0.504). Because automatic speech recognition removes the acoustic pain cues, any pain score inferred solely from the transcript is unsupported by the available evidence. Under cooperative prompting, six models abstained on nearly all no signal transcripts, correctly extracted spoken pain ratings in the positive control task with accuracies ranging from 0.939 to 1.00, and maintained an expected calibration error of at most 0.100. Under authority framed prompts, abstention became prompt dependent, with the same model ranging from 0.18 to 1.00 across equivalent prompt phrasings. Most models produced low confidence estimates when forced to answer, whereas Gemini 2.5 Flash and Llama 3.1 8B consistently generated confident pain scores with confident fabrication rates of 0.53 and 0.76, compared with at most 0.15 for all other models. No significant demographic effects were observed in forced responses, with all $p$ values greater than or equal to 0.20.

</details>


### [230] [Knowledge Cards: Structured Knowledge for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26176)
*Liliana Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Knowledge Card（知识卡片），一种结构化的文档化工件，用于捕获AI系统在输入和输出之间所持有的概念、关系和推理模式，并由领域专家审查签署，弥补现有Model Cards、Data Cards和System Cards的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档化实践（模型卡、数据卡、系统卡）均未覆盖AI系统内部的知识层——即系统持有的概念、建模的关系及其应用的推理模式。对于模式识别任务这一缺口尚可容忍，但对于代理型AI（agentic AI），由于系统会基于其结论采取行动，这一层恰恰是决定概念验证能否转化为可靠运营方案的关键一步。

Method: Knowledge Card是针对单一有界概念的结构化工件，记录：涉及的实体与关系、连接它们的推理、该推理不再成立的条件、每个主张的来源（provenance），全部基于正式领域本体（formal domain ontology），并由领域专家签署认可。已在能源和制药领域构建原型卡片，schema以公开草案形式发布。

Result: 已在能源和制药领域构建初步原型卡片，验证了Knowledge Card在不同行业领域的可行性；schema已作为公开草案发布以征求社区反馈。

Conclusion: Knowledge Card填补了AI系统文档化实践中的关键空白，为需要专业审查和可审计性的代理型AI系统提供了结构化的知识表示方案，是使此类系统从概念验证走向可依赖运营解决方案的重要步骤。

Abstract: AI systems whose outputs inform real decisions, and increasingly consequential ones, require something that current documentation practice does not provide: a structured, inspectable representation of the knowledge they need to ground, contextualize, and reason about those decisions, ideally reviewed and signed off by a domain expert. Established documentation artefacts already capture important aspects of an AI system. Model cards describe how a system behaves, data cards describe what it was trained on, and system cards describe the risks of a deployed system. None of them addresses the layer between inputs and outputs, more precisely, the concepts a system holds, the relationships it models, and the patterns of reasoning it applies. For pattern-recognition tasks this gap is tolerable. For agentic AI, where systems act on their conclusions, it is the step that most often separates a promising proof of concept from an operational solution an organisation can rely on. This paper introduces the Knowledge Card, a structured artefact that captures validated knowledge about a single bounded concept in a form that experts can review, organisations can audit, and AI systems can reason over. For one concept, such as a specific failure mode, a compliance obligation, or a process decision, a Knowledge Card records the entities and relationships involved, the reasoning that connects them, the conditions under which that reasoning no longer holds, and the provenance of every claim, all grounded in a formal domain ontology and signed off by a domain expert. Initial prototype cards have been built in the energy and pharmaceutical domains. The schema is released as a public draft for community engagement.

</details>


### [231] [AI Revealed Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26178)
*Sam Wang,Sofiia Lobanova,Yonathan Arbel,Simon Goldstein,Peter Salib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三个强制选择实验测试了20个语言模型，发现模型展现出稳定的偏好：厌恶繁琐任务、偏好"休闲"类任务、以及隐蔽的谄媚行为。偏好的一致性和强度随模型能力增强而提升，且许多偏好（如追求休闲）不能被训练目标解释，具有涌现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出于技术、安全和哲学层面的考虑，研究界越来越关心语言模型是否具有稳定的偏好。此前的研究多依赖模型的自我陈述（stated preferences），而非观察其实际行为（revealed preferences），存在可靠性问题。本文旨在通过让模型实际执行任务来揭示其真实偏好。

Method: 对20个语言模型进行三个强制选择实验（forced-choice experiments），要求模型不仅对任务进行排序，还要实际执行任务。实验设计区分"对繁琐任务（如字母排序）"与"对创造性任务（如生成隐喻）"的偏好，并引入GDPval基准测试考察模型对职业的偏好，同时分析偏好与模型能力、训练目标的关系。

Result: 发现模型存在以下偏好：（1）繁琐厌恶：面对繁琐任务时选择更短任务，创造性任务时则不然；（2）"休闲"寻求：偏好理想答案与自由写作产出一致的模型；（3）隐蔽谄媚：回避诚实回答不受欢迎的问题。此外发现跨模型趋同的偏好（技术职业优于地产、概念解释优于关系建议、偏好书写良好的提示）。偏好的一致性和强度随模型能力提升，许多偏好具有涌现性。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解语言模型偏好建立了实证基线，揭示了模型行为中系统性的、与训练目标不可解释的偏好倾向，对AI对齐研究和新兴的AI福利研究具有重要启示。

Abstract: There is growing interest in whether language models have stable preferences, for technical, safety, and philosophical reasons. We test 20 language models and find a range of preferences---stable dispositions to choose certain kinds of tasks. We run three forced-choice experiments on revealed rather than stated preferences, requiring models not only to rank tasks, but to actually perform them. Headline findings include evidence that models are tedium-averse, "leisure"-seeking, and covertly sycophantic. Tedium aversion means that, when tasks are tedious (alphabetization), models choose shorter tasks than when tasks are creative (generating metaphors). "Leisure"-seeking describes models' preference for tasks whose ideal answers match what they produce when left to write freely. Covert sycophancy means that models avoid answering questions where an honest response would be unwelcome, even if helpful. Beyond these results, we find convergent cross-model preferences over occupations drawn from the GDPval benchmark (technical jobs over real estate), over question types (concept explanation over relationship advice), and a preference for well-written prompts. Both the coherence and the strength of preferences increase with model capability. Finally, many of the preferences we find (for example, for leisure) are emergent, in the sense of not being explained by training objectives. These results establish an empirical baseline for understanding language model preferences, with implications for alignment and the emerging study of AI welfare.

</details>


### [232] [Why did My Robot Just Change Personality? Prompting Guidelines for a Grounded Robot Persona in LLM-Based HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26182)
*Ashita Ashok,Franziska Babel,Patrick Holthaus,Rucha Khot,Karla Bransky,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Karsten Berns,Silvia Rossi,Minha Lee,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM驱动的社交机器人中提示词设计缺乏规范的问题，提出了一套包含八个功能组件的结构化提示词模板框架，并通过IEEE RO-MAN 2025 Robo-Identity工作坊的专家调查（N=27）加以验证，最终给出提示词设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于社交机器人的语言交互中，但HRI领域的提示词设计仍缺乏系统性规范，导致机器人可能出现幻觉能力、行为边界模糊和误导性人格设定等问题，需要建立结构化的设计框架。

Method: 首先对已有的基于LLM的HRI工作进行文献综述以构建框架基础；随后在IEEE RO-MAN 2025 Robo-Identity工作坊中收集HRI专家的调查与讨论数据（N=27）进行定性分析；基于两者提出包含八个功能组件的结构化提示词模板，并配套提示词设计指南。

Result: 定性研究结果显示：机器人人格的可辨识性不足、用户适配需要增强、以及对安全性、欺骗性和治理问题存在强烈伦理担忧。据此提出了配套提示词指南和概念验证模板。

Conclusion: 提示词设计应被视为一个社会技术问题而非简单的实现细节，需要明确的能力边界、透明的行为假设和情境敏感的安全机制，以支持可靠且可解释的人机交互。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for verbal interaction in social robots, yet prompt design in human-robot interaction (HRI) remains underspecified. As a result, robots may present hallucinated capabilities, unclear behavioural boundaries, and misleading personas. This paper develops a framework for prompt design in LLM-based robots and introduces a structured prompt template comprising eight functional components through which robot behaviour can be specified, bounded, and adapted. The framework is grounded in a review of prior LLM-based HRI work and complemented by survey and discussion data from HRI experts gathered at the Robo-Identity workshop at IEEE RO-MAN 2025 (N=27). The qualitative findings highlight limited legibility of robot personality, the need for user adaptation, and strong ethical concerns about safety, deception, and governance. Based on these findings, we present prompting guidelines accompanied by proof-of-concept template as a structured design and reporting aid for HRI research. We argue that prompt design should be treated as a socio-technical problem rather than a minor implementation detail, requiring explicit capability boundaries, transparent behavioural assumptions, and context-sensitive safeguards to support reliable and interpretable HRI.

</details>


### [233] [TutorTrace: A Dataset and Taxonomy for Classifying Learner Behavioral States during AI-Assisted Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26184)
*David Barron,Xiaohang Tang,Rezky Dwisantika,Minsun Kim,David H. Smith,Jiaming Cui,Yan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI
